# PART 17 — FAQ Bank, Acceptance Tests & Chatbot Operations

*241 question-and-answer pairs, 43 acceptance tests, answer templates and refresh rules — every answer mapped to an official MRDU source.*

## FAQ Bank (241 pairs)

Every answer below is drawn from an official MRDU page or document; the source is named in the answer. Categories run in the order published by the institution.

### Admissions

**Q. How do I apply for admission at MRDU?**

Fill the online application via the Apply Now route at mrdu.edu.in/admissions/apply. The four published steps are: (1) Register & apply — fill the enquiry form with your programme preference and contact details; (2) Verify via email — the admissions team confirms your application and shares the document checklist; (3) Counselling — attend counselling as per schedule and carry originals for verification; (4) Fee payment — pay the prescribed fee to confirm your seat allocation.

**Q. Are admissions open for 2026-27?**

Yes. 'Admissions Open 2026-27' was notified on 22 May 2026 and the university is inviting applications for UG & PG programmes across 8 schools. Source: mrdu.edu.in/notifications and mrdu.edu.in/admissions.

**Q. What is the admission process?**

The process is: online registration and application, email verification by the admissions team with a document checklist, counselling attendance with original documents, seat allocation against sanctioned intake, and fee payment to confirm admission. Lateral entry, Ph.D. and other routes follow the same core sequence with programme-specific eligibility.

**Q. What are the eligibility criteria for B.Tech?**

Admission to B.Tech is based on JEE, state entrance ranks, MRUCET, or merit as stated in the official FAQ. The base eligibility is 10+2 (Intermediate) with Mathematics, Physics and Chemistry. Programme-specific eligibility is confirmed by the admissions office at the email-verification stage.

**Q. Is lateral entry available at MRDU?**

Yes. B.Tech Lateral Entry Admissions 2026-2027 are open (notified 22 May 2026; also published as a news item on 01 Feb 2026). The lateral entry page is mrdu.edu.in/admissions/lateral-entry. It is intended for diploma holders and eligible B.Sc graduates seeking direct entry into the second year of B.Tech.

**Q. What documents are required for admission?**

Carry originals plus photocopies of: Class 10 and Class 12 marks memoranda and certificates; entrance rank card/hall ticket; Transfer Certificate; Migration Certificate (if applicable); study/conduct certificate; Aadhaar or government photo ID; passport-size photographs; caste/category certificate if claiming reservation; income certificate for scholarship claims; degree/diploma certificates for PG and lateral entry; gap certificate if applicable; anti-ragging undertaking (mandatory); and a medical fitness certificate. The admissions team issues the definitive checklist by email during Stage 2.

**Q. Do I need to pay to confirm my seat?**

Yes. Fee payment is the final step of the admission process and confirms seat allocation. Programme-wise tuition, registration and other charges are published for each academic year and the university advises verifying the latest notification from the admissions office before payment.

**Q. Can I choose the Tirupati campus while applying?**

Yes. The online application form has a Campus selector with 'Hyderabad' and 'Tirupati' options. The Tirupati off-campus offers graduate programmes, undergraduate programmes, doctoral degrees and international hubs.

**Q. How long does the university take to respond to an application?**

The admissions page commits to counselling support by email and phone with a response within 2 business days.

**Q. Is there a single application deadline?**

The university does not publish a single universal deadline; counselling rounds are scheduled by the admissions office. Check the Notifications page (mrdu.edu.in/notifications) or contact the admissions office on +91 87120 20867 / info@mrdu.edu.in for the current round's deadline.

**Q. What entrance exams does MRDU accept?**

For B.Tech: JEE, state entrance ranks (e.g. EAPCET) or merit. For Ph.D.: the university's own MRDUCET entrance examination plus research interview, with direct entry for UGC-NET / CSIR-NET / GATE / JRF holders. For MBA/MCA/M.Tech: as per the published admissions process, typically entrance or merit based.

**Q. Does MRDU accept NRI admissions?**

MRDU operates admissions across 8 schools and 2 campuses and the application form captures state, city and campus. NRI candidates should contact the admissions office on +91 87120 20867 / info@mrdu.edu.in for the NRI-specific fee structure and document requirements, since the public fees page publishes only general UG/PG figures.

**Q. How do international students apply?**

The Tirupati off-campus explicitly offers 'International Hubs' (global collaborations, exchange programs, international academic exposure) and welcomes 'Global Students' from diverse cultures. International applicants should contact the Tirupati campus on 9247962726/28/29 or info@mrdu.edu.in.

**Q. Is there an admissions helpline?**

Yes — Admissions: +91 87120 20867; General: +91 93481 61303; Email: info@mrdu.edu.in.

**Q. Where is the application form?**

The application/enquiry form is at mrdu.edu.in/admissions/apply (also anchored at mrdu.edu.in/admissions#apply). Fields include first and last name, email, phone with +91 prefix, state, city, campus, school, programme and an optional message.

**Q. What is the admission brochure and where can I get it?**

The official MRDU Single Flyer is published at mrdu.edu.in/uploads/2026/02/MRDU_Single-Flyer.pdf. A 'Download brochure' link is also provided on the admissions page.

**Q. Can I apply offline?**

The published application route is online. For offline assistance, contact the admissions office on +91 87120 20867 or visit the campus at Maisammaguda (H), Gundlapochampally Village, Medchal Mandal, Medchal-Malkajgiri District, Telangana 500100.

**Q. Are anti-ragging and other undertakings required at admission?**

Yes. MRDU maintains a strict anti-ragging policy in accordance with UGC regulations and all students must submit the anti-ragging undertaking as per national guidelines (mrdu.edu.in/student-life/anti-ragging).

### Courses

**Q. What courses are available at MRDU?**

Undergraduate: B.Tech in CSE, Artificial Intelligence & ML, Data Science, Cyber Security, IoT, Information Technology, Electronics & Communication, Electrical & Electronics, Civil, Mechanical and Mining; plus BBA, BCA, B.Sc and B.Com. Postgraduate: M.Tech (CSE, VLSI & Embedded Systems, Structural Engineering, Electrical Power Systems), MBA and MCA. Doctoral: Ph.D. across 10 disciplines. Value-added courses and certifications (including AWS Academy) are also offered.

**Q. How many programmes and seats does MRDU have?**

MRDU reports 3,480 sanctioned seats in total, including 11 undergraduate programmes with 3,030 intake. The About page intake table lists CSE 960, AI & ML 720, Data Science 720, Cyber Security 120, ECE 180, EEE 60 and Mechanical 30.

**Q. What is the duration of the B.Tech programme?**

B.Tech is a 4-year, 8-semester undergraduate programme.

**Q. What is the duration of MBA and MCA?**

Both MBA and MCA are 2-year, 4-semester postgraduate programmes.

**Q. What is the duration of BBA and BCA?**

BBA and BCA are 3-year undergraduate programmes with year-wise academic calendars published (BBA I, II and III Year).

**Q. What specializations are offered in B.Tech CSE?**

Core Computer Science & Engineering plus specializations in Artificial Intelligence & Machine Learning, Data Science, Cyber Security and Internet of Things. Information Technology is a separate B.Tech programme.

**Q. What M.Tech specializations are offered?**

M.Tech in Computer Science & Engineering (intake 12), M.Tech VLSI and Embedded Systems (intake 18), M.Tech Structural Engineering (intake 30) and M.Tech Electrical Power Systems (intake 18).

**Q. What is the approved intake for B.Tech CSE?**

The CSE department page and the B.Tech CSE programme page state an approved intake of 720. The institutional About page lists Computer Science & Engineering at 960 as part of the total sanctioned intake table, reflecting core plus allied CSE specializations. Confirm the current-year split with the admissions office.

**Q. What is the intake for B.Tech AIML and Data Science?**

720 each, per the institutional intake table and programme pages.

**Q. What is the intake for ECE, EEE and Mechanical?**

B.Tech ECE 180, B.Tech EEE 60 and Mechanical Engineering 30, per the institutional intake table. Cyber Security is listed at 120.

**Q. Which schools make up MRDU?**

Eight schools: School of Computer Science and Engineering (SCSE), School of Electronics and Electrical Engineering (SEEE), School of Civil and Mechanical Engineering (SCME), School of Management and Technology (SMGT), School of Computer Applications (SCAP), School of Sciences (SCIE), School of Humanities and Sciences (SH&S), plus the Freshman Engineering foundation unit.

**Q. Are the programmes accredited?**

Yes. NAAC A++ (Cycle III), NBA Tier-1 accreditation for eligible UG and PG engineering programmes (e.g. ECE and EEE for AY 2021-22 to 2022-23, CSE and allied UG programmes), AICTE-approved programmes, and affiliation to JNTU/JNTUH for the respective programmes.

**Q. Is there a B.Sc and B.Com programme?**

Yes. A 'B.Sc and B.Com Courses' notification was published on 11 Jul 2026 and the B.Com & B.Sc I Year academic calendar is published for 2026-2027. Details are available on the Notifications page.

**Q. What career opportunities does B.Tech CSE offer?**

Software developer, data scientist, ML engineer, cloud architect, cybersecurity analyst and allied roles. Graduates also pursue higher studies, research and entrepreneurship.

**Q. What career opportunities does ECE offer?**

Design Engineer, ASIC Engineer, Embedded Engineer, Network Support Engineer, Circuit Design Engineer, Wireless Communication Engineer and Signal Processing Engineer.

**Q. What career opportunities does EEE offer?**

Electrical Design Engineer, CAD Technician, Control and Instrumentation Engineer, Broadcast Engineer, Manufacturing Systems Engineer and Network Engineer.

**Q. What career opportunities does Civil Engineering offer?**

Structural Engineer, Geotechnical Consultant, Transportation Engineer, Environmental Engineer, Construction Manager and Site Engineer.

**Q. What career opportunities does MCA offer?**

Software architect, systems analyst, IT consultant, project manager, full-stack developer and research roles in computing.

**Q. Does MRDU offer MBA with analytics?**

Yes. The MBA blends strategy, finance, marketing, operations and business analytics, with internships, corporate mentoring, case studies and live industry projects.

**Q. Is there an IoT programme?**

Yes — B.Tech Computer Science & Engineering (IoT), delivered by the IoT department, with focus on embedded systems, sensor networks, edge computing and industrial IoT. The university homepage references a Microsoft IoT industry initiative.

**Q. Where can I see all programmes?**

The complete programme listing with detail pages is at mrdu.edu.in/academics/programs.

### Fees

**Q. What is the fee structure at MRDU?**

As per the official disclosure, tuition is around ₹1,15,000 per year for undergraduate programmes and around ₹60,000 per year for postgraduate programmes. These are indicative band figures for the academic year; programme-wise tuition, registration and other charges are published each academic year and the university advises verifying the latest notification from the admissions office before payment.

**Q. What is the B.Tech fee?**

Indicative undergraduate tuition is ₹1,15,000 per year (source: mrdu.edu.in/admissions/fees). Confirm the current-year amount with the admissions office on +91 87120 20867 before payment.

**Q. What is the MBA fee?**

Indicative postgraduate tuition is ₹60,000 per year (source: mrdu.edu.in/admissions/fees). Confirm the current-year amount with the admissions office.

**Q. What is the M.Tech fee?**

Indicative postgraduate tuition is ₹60,000 per year. Verify the programme-specific figure from the admissions office.

**Q. What is the MCA fee?**

Indicative postgraduate tuition is ₹60,000 per year. Verify with the admissions office.

**Q. What is the BCA fee?**

BCA sits within the undergraduate band of approximately ₹1,15,000 per year as an indicative figure. Programmes like BCA, BBA, B.Sc and B.Com may have programme-specific amounts — confirm with the admissions office.

**Q. What is the B.Sc fee?**

B.Sc is an undergraduate programme; the indicative undergraduate tuition band is ₹1,15,000 per year. The 'B.Sc and B.Com Courses' notification (11 Jul 2026) and the admissions office give the exact programme fee.

**Q. What is the Ph.D. fee?**

The Ph.D. fee is published in the Ph.D. notification and the MRDU Ph.D Regulations 2025-26 rather than on the general fees page. Check mrdu.edu.in/research/phd and mrdu.edu.in/phdadmissions, or contact the research section via info@mrdu.edu.in.

**Q. What is the hostel fee?**

Hostel fees are notified separately from tuition — the fees page states 'Hostel, transport, and other fees are notified separately.' Contact the hostel administration on +91 93481 61303 or info@mrdu.edu.in, or check mrdu.edu.in/notifications.

**Q. What is the transport fee?**

Transport fees are notified separately and depend on the route/stage. Contact the transport office on +91 93481 61303.

**Q. What is the examination fee?**

Examination fees are published per examination cycle as dedicated Exam Fee Notification PDFs on mrdu.edu.in/examinations/fees. Recent notifications include B.Tech/BBA Semester-III Regular (MR25), B.Tech I-IV Supplementary November 2026, and M.Tech/MBA Sem-II (MR25) July 2026.

**Q. What is the application fee?**

The application fee is collected through the official online application route. The definitive amount for the 2026-27 cycle is published in the admissions notification and confirmed by the admissions office at the email-verification stage.

**Q. Are there other charges besides tuition?**

Yes — registration, examination, hostel, transport and other charges are notified separately. The fees page publishes tuition and related charges by programme level and directs students to the respective offices for hostel, transport and other fees.

**Q. What are the refund rules?**

Refund rules are governed by the university's fee and admission policy and by UGC/AICTE norms for the relevant admission year. The refund schedule with cancellation windows is published with the admission notification and confirmed by the accounts office via +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in.

**Q. How can I pay my fees?**

Payments are made through the official online payment route linked from the application and fee notifications, or at the university accounts office. Never pay to personal bank accounts or unofficial UPI IDs — always verify through official channels before payment.

**Q. Is the fee the same at the Tirupati campus?**

Campus-specific and programme-specific amounts are confirmed by the admissions office. Contact the Tirupati campus on 9247962726/28/29 or info@mrdu.edu.in for campus-specific fees.

**Q. Are fee concessions available?**

Yes — MRDU offers scholarships and fee concessions subject to academic performance, entrance ranks and applicable government schemes. See mrdu.edu.in/admissions/scholarships.

**Q. Where can I see the latest fee notification?**

On mrdu.edu.in/admissions/fees (tuition) and mrdu.edu.in/examinations/fees (examination fees), and on the Notifications page.

### Scholarships

**Q. What scholarships does MRDU offer?**

MRDU offers (1) merit scholarships for top rank holders in qualifying examinations and entrance tests, (2) sports and cultural achievement awards, and (3) support aligned to eligible state and central government schemes where applicable.

**Q. How do I apply for a scholarship?**

Complete the online application to be considered for aid (mrdu.edu.in/admissions/apply), then submit the scholarship or fee-concession request with supporting documents through the admissions or accounts office. Approval and disbursement are notified by the accounts/scholarship section.

**Q. What documents are needed for a scholarship?**

Scholarship application form, Class 10 and 12 marks memoranda, qualifying/entrance rank card, income certificate, caste/category certificate where applicable, Aadhaar, bank account details in the student's name, bonafide certificate/admission confirmation, current-year fee receipt, and sports/cultural achievement certificates for those awards.

**Q. What is the scholarship amount?**

Scholarship amounts are announced each academic year on the Notifications page and in the scholarship notification for that year. Beneficiary and disbursement data is disclosed in the institutional AQAR reports. Confirm the current quantum with the scholarship/accounts section.

**Q. What are the government scholarships available?**

MRDU students are eligible for eligible state and central government scholarship schemes, subject to the notified eligibility for each scheme. Documentation of scholarship beneficiaries is disclosed in the AQAR reports. The SC/ST Cell facilitates scholarship applications (mrdu.edu.in/student-life/sc-st-cell).

**Q. Is there a scholarship deadline?**

Scholarship deadlines are announced per academic year on the Notifications page. Government scheme deadlines follow the respective state/central portal schedule, typically within the first semester after admission.

**Q. Are sports scholarships available?**

Yes — the university offers sports and cultural achievement awards as a scholarship category (mrdu.edu.in/admissions/scholarships).

**Q. Who handles scholarship facilitation on campus?**

The SC/ST Cell facilitates scholarships, grievance support and compliance with reservation roster requirements, and the Equal Opportunity Cell ensures equitable access to academic resources, scholarships and facilities.

### Hostel

**Q. Is hostel accommodation available at MRDU?**

Yes. MRDU provides on-campus hostel accommodation for boys and girls. The hostel page is mrdu.edu.in/student-life/hostels.

**Q. Where is the hostel located?**

The hostels are located on the main campus at Maisammaguda (H), Gundlapochampally Village, Medchal Mandal, Medchal-Malkajgiri District, Telangana 500100.

**Q. What is the hostel fee?**

Hostel fees are notified separately from tuition. Contact the hostel administration on +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in, or check the Notifications page for the current 2026-27 hostel fee by room type.

**Q. What room types are available in the hostel?**

Hostel accommodation is offered across sharing types (commonly single, double and triple occupancy) with attached or shared facilities. The exact room type matrix and availability for the current year is issued by the hostel administration at allotment.

**Q. Is mess facility included in the hostel?**

Yes. Hostel messes provide scheduled breakfast, lunch, snacks and dinner with a rotating menu covering vegetarian and multi-cuisine options, and mess facilities are covered within the hostel fee structure.

**Q. What are the hostel rules?**

Hostel rules govern entry and exit timings with late-entry permission procedures, visitor and guest policy, room allotment and retention, cleanliness and maintenance responsibilities, prohibited items, ragging prohibition (zero tolerance), leave and out-station intimation, and the disciplinary procedure. The rules document is issued by the hostel administration at admission.

**Q. What are the hostel timings?**

Hostel and mess timings — entry/exit cut-off, study hours, mess service windows and visitor hours — are published on the hostel notice boards and in the hostel rules document issued at admission.

**Q. Who is the hostel warden?**

Each hostel is supervised by a residential Warden and assistant wardens responsible for discipline, welfare, emergency response and room administration. Warden names and contact numbers are published on the hostel notice boards and given to residents at allotment; for the current contact call +91 93481 61303.

**Q. What facilities are available in the hostel?**

Furnished rooms with study furniture, Wi-Fi, mess and dining hall, common rooms and recreation areas, laundry services, hot water and power backup, security with warden supervision, access to campus sports and gym, and medical support with an ambulance on call.

**Q. Is there Wi-Fi in the hostels?**

Yes — hostel Wi-Fi is provided under the campus-wide connectivity framework and governed by the university's IT Policy.

**Q. Is laundry available in the hostel?**

Yes — laundry services are available to hostel residents (in-house or outsourced), with charges notified by the hostel office.

**Q. How is hostel security handled?**

Hostel security includes warden supervision, security staff, visitor control, entry/exit monitoring and CCTV coverage. Girls' hostels have dedicated enhanced security protocols. Ragging and harassment are covered by the Anti-Ragging Committee and the ICC.

**Q. Is medical support available in the hostel?**

Yes — residents have access to the campus ambulance service and to subsidized treatment at Malla Reddy Narayana Multi Speciality Hospital about 6 km from campus, plus first-aid support within campus.

**Q. How do I apply for a hostel room?**

(1) Confirm your admission; (2) submit the hostel application/allotment request to the hostel administration during admission or at the start of the academic year; (3) pay the notified hostel fee to confirm the allotment; (4) report to the allotted hostel with the allotment slip and documents at the warden's office; (5) sign the hostel rules undertaking.

**Q. Are hostel seats guaranteed?**

Hostel seats are limited and allotted on a first-come basis after admission confirmation. Availability by room type is confirmed by the hostel office at the time of application.

**Q. What is the hostel contact number?**

Hostel administration: +91 93481 61303 · Email: info@mrdu.edu.in. National anti-ragging helpline for any hostel-related ragging complaint: 1800-180-5522.

### Transport

**Q. Does MRDU provide bus transport?**

Yes. MRDU operates institutional bus services connecting the Maisammaguda campus with Hyderabad city and surrounding localities, with scheduled morning pick-up and evening drop timings.

**Q. What are the bus routes and timings?**

Route and timing information is published by the transport office and displayed at the transport desk and hostel notice boards. Morning services are timed to reach campus before the first academic hour and evening services depart after the last academic hour, with additional services during examinations and major events.

**Q. What is the transport fee?**

Transport fees are notified separately from tuition and depend on the route/stage. Contact the transport office on +91 93481 61303 or info@mrdu.edu.in for the current 2026-27 route-wise fee.

**Q. Where are the bus stops?**

Named bus stops are defined along each route with fixed pick-up/drop points and timings. The stop list per route is published by the transport office and shared with registered transport users.

**Q. What are the transport rules?**

Transport rules cover carrying and displaying the transport pass, boarding only at designated stops, no standing on footboards, discipline expectations, prohibition of smoking/consumption, damage liability and the loss-of-pass procedure. Repeated violations can lead to withdrawal of the transport facility.

**Q. Is transport available for the Tirupati campus?**

Campus transport arrangements for Tirupati are managed by the campus office — contact 9247962726/28/29 for details.

**Q. What is the emergency transport arrangement?**

A campus ambulance service is available for quick response in emergencies, and subsidized treatment is available at Malla Reddy Narayana Multi Speciality Hospital about 6 km from campus. Transport emergencies (breakdown/accident) should be reported immediately to the transport office and the campus security desk.

### Exams

**Q. When is my exam?**

Exam timetables are published at mrdu.edu.in/examinations/timetables and are specific to your programme, year, semester and regulation. Recent published timetables include B.Tech IV Year II (MR22) Advanced Supplementary September 2026, B.Tech II/III/IV Year I Sem Mid-I August 2026, M.Tech II Semester (MR25) Regular July 2026, and the B.Tech I-IV Year Supplementary April 2026 cycle. Always confirm your exact dates on that page or with your department examination coordinator, as timetables are revised.

**Q. Where can I get the exam timetable?**

At mrdu.edu.in/examinations/timetables, filtered by B.Tech, M.Tech, MBA and BBA sections. Timetables are also circulated through department notice boards and the student portal.

**Q. How do I pay the examination fee?**

Examination fee notifications are published as PDFs on mrdu.edu.in/examinations/fees. Each notification states the fee amount, the last date to pay without fine and the payment mode. Verify the latest notification before payment.

**Q. How can I apply for revaluation?**

(1) Check the RC/RV circular published on mrdu.edu.in/examinations/circulars after your result is declared; (2) pay the prescribed RC/RV fee within the window stated (separate last dates with and without fine may apply); (3) submit the application online or at the examination branch; (4) track status on the student portal. Recounting and revaluation are both available.

**Q. What is the difference between recounting and revaluation?**

Recounting verifies the totalling of marks and that all answers were evaluated. Revaluation involves a re-assessment of the answer script. Both are applied for after result publication as per the RC/RV circular, with prescribed fees.

**Q. What are internal exams?**

Internal assessment uses Mid Examinations I and II per semester. Published internal timetables include B.Tech I/II/III/IV Year Mid-I and Mid-II exams, M.Tech Mid examinations, MBA Mid examinations and BBA Mid examinations. Internal marks contribute to the final grade as defined in the academic regulations.

**Q. What is the attendance requirement for exams?**

Attendance requirements are set in the academic regulations and enforced before hall ticket issuance. A minimum attendance percentage is required for examination eligibility, with shortfalls addressed through condonation as per regulation. Confirm the exact threshold for your batch in the regulation document.

**Q. What happens if I have backlogs?**

Backlog subjects are cleared through supplementary examinations conducted for your regulation. Multi-regulation supplementary timetables (MR15 through MR25) are published so older backlog papers can be cleared. Grace marks provisions may apply — see the Grace Marks Circular (e.g. for MR22 regulation, 07 Sep 2026).

**Q. How do I get my hall ticket?**

Hall tickets are issued through the examination portal for registered candidates who have paid the examination fee and meet attendance eligibility. Release and download instructions are circulated with each exam fee notification.

**Q. How do I apply for supplementary exams?**

Supplementary examination notifications and timetables are published on mrdu.edu.in/examinations/timetables and /examinations/fees. Pay the applicable supplementary exam fee within the notified window and register through the examination portal or department coordinator.

**Q. What are the exam regulations?**

Examination conduct, evaluation weightings, grading and promotion are governed by the academic regulations of your regulation code (MR22, MR24, MR25 etc.), approved by the Academic Council. Exam instructions for candidates are also published (e.g. Exam Instructions B.Tech Sem-1).

**Q. Where is the examination cell and who do I contact?**

The examination section is contactable on +91 93481 61303 and info@mrdu.edu.in. Department-level queries route through your department examination coordinator.

**Q. What is grace marks and does it apply to me?**

Grace marks allow a limited marks concession to borderline candidates under conditions defined by the university. A 'Grace Marks Circular for MR22 Regulation' was published on 07 Sep 2026. Check the circular to see whether your regulation and case qualify.

**Q. How do I get a transcript or certificate?**

Transcripts and certificates are issued by the Office of the Controller of Examinations on application with the prescribed fee, quoting your name, programme, regulation, year of passing and hall ticket number. Contact +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in.

**Q. How can an employer verify my certificate?**

Certificate verification requests from employers and universities should be addressed to the examination section with the candidate's details, hall ticket/roll number and year of passing — contact +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in.

### Results

**Q. When will results be released?**

Results are declared by official circular and published on mrdu.edu.in/examinations/circulars. Results declaration circulars dated 22 Sep 2026 covered MBA and M.Tech Sem-II (MR25) Regular Examinations plus M.Tech Sem-I and MBA Sem-I Supplementary exams, and II MBA II Sem (MR24) Regular Examinations plus M.Tech and MBA all-regulation supplementary exams.

**Q. Where can I check my results?**

Results are published through the result circulars on the Examinations pages and can be retrieved from the student/examination portal using your registration number and semester.

**Q. How do I get my semester results?**

Semester results are declared in the result circular series and accessed through the student portal. The marks memo or grade sheet issued with the result is the authoritative record.

**Q. When are supplementary results declared?**

Supplementary results are declared in the same circular series as regular results — for example, the supplementary results for M.Tech and MBA across all regulations were declared on 22 Sep 2026.

**Q. How do I check my revaluation result?**

Revaluation outcomes are notified after the RC/RV window closes, through the same circular mechanism. RC/RV status can be tracked on the student portal.

**Q. What do I do if I cannot find my result?**

Contact the examination section with your hall ticket number and semester — +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in — or reach your department examination coordinator.

**Q. How is grading done at MRDU?**

MRDU follows an Outcome-Based Education (OBE) grade system with credit-weighted evaluation under the Choice Based Credit System (CBCS). Programme Outcomes, Programme Specific Outcomes and Course Outcomes are mapped to assessment. Letter grades, grade points and SGPA/CGPA computation are defined in the academic regulations for your regulation code.

### Placements

**Q. Which companies visit MRDU for placements?**

Published 2026-batch drives include Accenture (Associate Software Engineer), TCS (NQT), Infosys (Systems Engineer & Digital Specialist Engineer), Cognizant (GenC), Wipro (Elite NTH — Project Engineer), Capgemini (Exceller), Deloitte (Analyst Trainee) and ISRO (Technical Assistant Recruitment 2026 — 84 posts). Recruiters span IT, consulting, core engineering and government research organisations.

**Q. What is the placement process?**

(1) Register with the Training & Placement Cell / Career Development Centre; (2) attend pre-placement training — aptitude, technical and soft-skills training, mock interviews and group discussions; (3) attend pre-placement talks; (4) take company online assessments; (5) attend technical and HR interviews; (6) receive offers and progress to onboarding.

**Q. What is the placement percentage?**

The university's published institutional indicator is a 96% placement rate.

**Q. What is the highest package at MRDU?**

MRDU does not publish highest, average or median package figures on its public placement page. For verified salary data by branch and year, request the official placement report from the Training & Placement Cell. Do not rely on unofficial package claims.

**Q. What training is provided for placements?**

Pre-placement training includes aptitude training (quantitative aptitude, logical reasoning, verbal ability), technical training (programming, data structures, core-subject and domain skills), soft skills and communication (group discussions, presentations, business etiquette), mock interviews with feedback, and certification programmes such as AWS Academy.

**Q. What is the latest placement drive?**

Recent published drives include Accenture's Associate Software Engineer Campus Drive for the 2026 batch (28 May 2026) and TCS NQT registrations for the 2026 batch (20 May 2026). Check mrdu.edu.in/about/placements and the Notifications page (Placement category) for the newest drives.

**Q. Am I eligible for campus placements?**

Typical eligibility depends on academic performance (the CGPA/percentage threshold set by each recruiter), backlog status, attendance and discipline record, and registration with the placement cell. Recruiter-specific eligibility — percentage cut-off, branch and year of passing — is announced with each drive notification.

**Q. How do I register for placements?**

Students register with the Training & Placement Cell / Career Development Centre during the pre-final and final year. Registration drive announcements are published on the Notifications page under the Placement category and communicated by department placement coordinators.

**Q. Are internships available?**

Yes. Internships are embedded in the programmes and supported by the placement cell and industry ties. The university publishes an Internship Policy (19 pages) and reports student internships and field work/projects in its AQAR filings.

**Q. What job roles are offered to students?**

Published roles include Associate Software Engineer (Accenture), TCS NQT-based roles, Systems Engineer and Digital Specialist Engineer (Infosys), GenC roles (Cognizant), Project Engineer (Wipro), Capgemini Exceller, Analyst Trainee (Deloitte) and Technical Assistant (ISRO). Department career pathways add software developer, data scientist, ML engineer, cloud architect, cybersecurity analyst, design/ASIC/embedded engineer, structural/geotechnical/transportation/environmental engineer, construction manager and management roles.

**Q. Where is the placement report?**

Placement information is published at mrdu.edu.in/about/placements; placement performance is disclosed in the AQAR reports (2020-21 to 2023-24) and the SSR Cycle III. A formal placement report is available from the Training & Placement Cell on request.

**Q. Who do I contact for placement queries?**

Route placement queries through your department placement coordinator and the Training & Placement Cell — general line +91 93481 61303, email info@mrdu.edu.in, page mrdu.edu.in/about/placements.

Qualifications published in the directory include Ph.D., M.Tech, M.E., MBA, MCA, M.Sc., M.A. and M.Phil., with a large share of Professors and Associate Professors holding doctorates. The university reports 95% of faculty drawn from renowned national and international institutes.

Individual faculty email addresses and phone numbers are not published on the public directory. Route queries through the department office or the university line +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in.

### Departments

**Q. What departments are available at MRDU?**

Sixteen academic departments plus a freshman engineering unit: CSE, AIML, CSE-Cyber Security, CSE-Data Science, IoT, IT, ECE, EEE, Civil, Mechanical, Mining, MBA, BCA, MCA, Humanities & Sciences, Mathematics, and Freshman Engineering. BBA is also offered under Management and Technology.

**Q. What subjects are in CSE?**

Core subjects include data structures, object-oriented programming, database systems, cloud computing, operating systems, computer networks and allied electives, alongside mathematics, sciences and humanities foundation courses.

**Q. Where is the Mechanical Engineering department?**

All departments are located on the MRDU Main Campus, Maisammaguda (H), Gundlapochampally Village, Medchal Mandal, Medchal-Malkajgiri District, Telangana 500100. The Mechanical Engineering department page is mrdu.edu.in/academics/departments/mechanical.

**Q. What information is published for each department?**

Each department page carries About the Department, Head of the Department, Programmes, Syllabus, Faculty, Facilities, Library, Seminars, Research, PhD Scholars, Achievements, Lab Equipments and Photo Gallery.

**Q. What is the intake for the Civil Engineering department?**

B.Tech (Civil Engineering) approved intake is 60 and M.Tech (Structural Engineering) is 30. The programme is affiliated to JNTU and approved by AICTE, with NBA Tier-1 accreditation.

**Q. What is the intake for ECE?**

B.Tech (Electronics and Communication Engineering) 180 and M.Tech (VLSI and Embedded Systems) 18, with NBA Tier-1 accreditation for AY 2021-22 to 2022-23.

**Q. What is the intake for EEE?**

B.Tech (Electrical and Electronics Engineering) 60 and M.Tech (Electrical Power Systems) 18, with NBA Tier-1 accreditation for AY 2021-22 to 2022-23.

**Q. What is the intake for M.Tech CSE?**

12 seats, per the CSE department page.

**Q. Which school does the MBA department belong to?**

The School of Management and Technology (SMGT), which also houses BBA.

**Q. Which school does Mining Engineering belong to?**

Mining engineering is grouped with the School of Sciences, which provides an interdisciplinary foundation in humanities, mathematics and mining disciplines.

**Q. Does MRDU have an IoT department?**

Yes — a dedicated Internet of Things department delivering the B.Tech CSE (IoT) programme, with 6 faculty listed in the directory.

**Q. What is the Humanities and Sciences department for?**

The School of Humanities & Sciences and its H&S department deliver mathematics, physics, chemistry, English and communication courses underpinning all engineering programmes, plus the Freshman Engineering (FE) first-year foundation. 11 faculty are listed under H&S and 22 under Mathematics.

### Research

**Q. What research facilities does MRDU have?**

MRDU reports 650+ research scholars, 80+ cutting-edge laboratories, UGC-recognized R&D centres, a Centre of Excellence, and a 4-Star IIC rating. Research tooling includes LabVIEW, IBM and HP DevOps stacks, Cadence, Ansys, Staad Pro and gas testing facilities.

**Q. What research projects are running at MRDU?**

Research projects run at department level and through the R&D Cell and UGC-recognized R&D centres. Funding routes include AICTE schemes (the institution reports ₹50 Lakh AICTE Margadarshan Scheme support), internal seed grants, consultancy and industry-sponsored/collaborative projects. Departmental details are on each department's Research tab.

**Q. What research policies exist?**

The Research Policy, Consultancy Policy, Code of Ethics, Incentives Policy and Seed Grant Policy — all published at mrdu.edu.in/research/policies. The IPR Policy and IEC Guidelines are also published.

**Q. How many research scholars are there?**

The university reports 650+ research scholars, listed department-wise under the PhD Scholars tab on each department page.

**Q. What patents does the university have?**

Patents are tracked by the R&D Cell and disclosed in AQAR and NIRF submissions, governed by the IPR Policy which covers invention disclosure, ownership, filing support and revenue sharing. For the verified current patent list, contact the R&D Cell — do not rely on secondary counts.

**Q. Are there research grants for faculty?**

Yes — the Seed Grant Policy provides internal seed funding for faculty research initiation, the Incentives Policy rewards publications, patents and funded projects, and external funding comes via DST/SERB, AICTE, UGC and industry collaboration.

**Q. What is the EDC?**

The Entrepreneurship Development Cell runs entrepreneurship programming. Its published activities include the inauguration of the Agri-Social Entrepreneurship Cell and Women Entrepreneurship Cell (12 Sep 2026), the CYBERPRENEURS talk (21 Aug 2026), MSME Idea Hackathon 6.0 and IGNITE 2026 innovation and entrepreneurship summit.

**Q. What is the BIC?**

The Business Incubation Centre (mrdu.edu.in/research/bic) incubates student and faculty startups within the university's innovation ecosystem, working alongside the Institution's Innovation Council (4-Star) and the EDC.

**Q. Does MRDU have industry collaborations?**

Yes. MRDU maintains strong industry ties through IIIC (Industry Institute Interaction Cell), MoUs including an international collaboration MoU signed in 2026, industry-sponsored design projects, internships, guest lectures, AWS Academy and Microsoft IoT programmes, with 1000+ collaborative activities reported per year.

**Q. What are the research areas at MRDU?**

Computing (AI/ML, data science, cyber security, IoT, cloud, software engineering); Electronics & Electrical (VLSI, embedded systems, communication, signal processing, power systems, renewables, smart grids); Civil & Mechanical (structural engineering, sustainable construction, materials, smart infrastructure, thermal systems, manufacturing, robotics); Sciences & Mathematics; and Management (business analytics, strategy, entrepreneurship, digital transformation).

### Ph.D

**Q. What Ph.D. programmes does MRDU offer?**

Ph.D. programmes across 10 disciplines for 2026-27 under UGC Ph.D. Regulations 2022, including Computer Science & Engineering, Electronics & Communication Engineering, Electrical & Electronics Engineering, Civil Engineering and Mechanical Engineering, plus Sciences and Management disciplines.

**Q. How can I apply for Ph.D. at MRDU?**

(1) Read the Ph.D. Programme Notification 2026-27 and the MRDU Ph.D Regulations 2025-26; (2) confirm eligibility (master's degree; NET/GATE/JRF for direct entry); (3) submit the online application at mrdu.edu.in/phdadmissions; (4) appear for MRDUCET (unless claiming direct entry) and the research interview; (5) on selection pay the prescribed Ph.D. fee; (6) complete coursework in Research Methodology & Ethics and begin research under an allotted supervisor.

**Q. What is MRDUCET?**

MRDUCET is the MRDU Common Entrance Test — the university's doctoral entrance examination. The notification 'Ph.D Entrance (MRDUCET) Examination Date Announced' is pinned on the university homepage and notifications feed; check mrdu.edu.in/phdadmissions for the announced date, syllabus, hall ticket and centre details.

**Q. What are the Ph.D. eligibility criteria?**

A master's degree (or equivalent) in the relevant discipline from a recognised university with the minimum prescribed marks/grade, plus qualification in MRDUCET followed by a research interview — or direct entry for candidates with UGC-NET / CSIR-NET / GATE / JRF qualifications, per UGC Ph.D. Regulations 2022.

**Q. What is the Ph.D. selection framework?**

The published selection framework is 70 : 30, alongside the entrance examination and research interview route under UGC Ph.D. Regulations 2022.

**Q. Can I do a part-time Ph.D. at MRDU?**

Yes — the Ph.D. admissions page advertises both full-time and part-time doctoral research opportunities.

**Q. What are the Ph.D. disciplines offered?**

Ten disciplines for 2026-27, spanning Engineering & Technology (CSE, ECE, EEE, Civil, Mechanical) and Sciences/Management streams, as listed on mrdu.edu.in/phdadmissions.

**Q. Who are the Ph.D. supervisors?**

Supervisors are drawn from Ph.D.-qualified Professors and Associate Professors. The supervisor list by discipline is published with the Ph.D. notification and R&D Cell communications; scholar-supervisor allocation follows UGC Regulations 2022 norms.

**Q. What does Ph.D. coursework involve?**

Comprehensive coursework including Research Methodology and Ethics, as stated on the Ph.D. admissions page, followed by research under an allotted supervisor.

**Q. What are the Ph.D. regulations?**

The MRDU Ph.D Regulations 2025-26, published under 'Latest Regulations' on mrdu.edu.in/research/phd, govern doctoral admission, coursework, supervisor allocation, progress monitoring, publication requirements, thesis submission and viva-voce, consistent with UGC Ph.D. Regulations 2022.

**Q. Who do I contact for Ph.D. queries?**

Via the research page mrdu.edu.in/research, the Ph.D. admissions page mrdu.edu.in/phdadmissions, email info@mrdu.edu.in or phone +91 93481 61303.

### Campus

**Q. Where is Malla Reddy University?**

The main campus is at Maisammaguda (H), Gundlapochampally Village, Medchal Mandal, Medchal-Malkajgiri District, Telangana State — 500100, on the Kompally–Bahadurpally Road, Hyderabad. There is also an off-campus at No. 6, Krishnapuram Village, Renigunta Mandal, Tirupati District, Andhra Pradesh 517520.

**Q. Where is the hostel?**

The hostels are on the main campus at Maisammaguda, Hyderabad. See mrdu.edu.in/student-life/hostels.

**Q. Is there a gym on campus?**

Yes — modern gym facilities are reported in the institutional infrastructure disclosures, alongside 24X7 library access, as part of campus student life.

**Q. What sports facilities are available?**

Sports facilities include the MRDU Sports Ground (host of Sports Arena 2026, 08-10 October 2026), indoor and outdoor games facilities, and campus sports programming integrated with KALOTSAVAM. Sports achievement awards are offered under the scholarship framework.

**Q. Is transport available?**

Yes — MRDU operates bus services on defined routes connecting campus with Hyderabad and surrounding areas, with morning pick-up and evening drop timings, plus a campus ambulance for emergencies.

**Q. Where is the library?**

The Central Library is on the main campus at Maisammaguda, Hyderabad. Page: mrdu.edu.in/academics/library.

**Q. Is there an auditorium?**

Yes — a Major Auditorium (used for orientation, graduation and major events) and a Mini Auditorium (used for awareness sessions and committee programmes).

**Q. Is Wi-Fi available on campus?**

Yes — campus-wide Wi-Fi connectivity is reported in institutional disclosures, governed by the university's IT Policy.

**Q. What medical facilities are on campus?**

An ambulance service is available for quick emergency response, and subsidized medical facilities are extended through Malla Reddy Narayana Multi Speciality Hospital about 6 km from campus. Page: mrdu.edu.in/student-life/health.

**Q. Is the campus accessible for differently-abled students?**

Yes. The university publishes PCS (Physically Challenged Support) facilities: ramps on all major academic and administrative buildings (Ramp 1-5), lift facilities in multi-storey buildings (Elevator 1-2), and accessible washrooms (Washroom 1-2), forming a barrier-free campus.

**Q. Where is the Tirupati campus?**

No. 6, Krishnapuram Village, Renigunta Mandal, Tirupati District, Andhra Pradesh 517520. Phone 9247962726/28/29. The nearest railway stations are Renigunta Junction and Tirupati Main, and the nearest airport is Tirupati International Airport.

**Q. What is the nearest airport and railway station to the main campus?**

For the Hyderabad main campus, the nearest airport is Rajiv Gandhi International Airport (HYD), Shamshabad, and the principal railway stations are Secunderabad Junction and Hyderabad Deccan, with Medchal as the nearest local station on the Hyderabad–Medchal line.

### Library

**Q. What is in the MRDU library?**

The Central Library offers 50,000+ volumes and digital resources (institutional disclosures report 60,094 volumes and 6,979 titles) with AICTE/UGC-conforming collections, digital subscriptions, reading halls and 24X7 access.

**Q. What digital resources does the library subscribe to?**

IEEE, DELNET, J-Gate, ASTM Digital Library, INFLIBNET N-LIST, e-ATL and the Knowledge Hub engineering e-library (k-hub), plus SWAYAM/NPTEL access under the SWAYAM-NPTEL Policy.

**Q. What are the library timings?**

Library timings are published by the Central Library; the institution reports 24X7 library access in its infrastructure disclosures, with reading halls and digital access available across extended hours.

**Q. What are the library rules?**

Library rules are governed by the Library Policy (22 pages) covering membership, borrowing limits and loan periods, renewals, overdue fines, loss/damage charges, conduct in reading halls, digital resource usage and licensing, copyright and plagiarism, and disciplinary action.

**Q. Can I access IEEE and DELNET from home?**

IEEE, DELNET, J-Gate, ASTM, INFLIBNET N-LIST and other subscribed e-resources are accessible through the library's digital route with member credentials issued by the Central Library. Off-campus access is provided via the subscribed platform.

**Q. Are there department libraries?**

Yes — each department has library provision as part of its facilities, shown on the department 'Library' tab, holding core texts, reference material and project reports for that discipline.

**Q. How do I get library membership?**

Membership is available to students, research scholars, faculty and staff on production of the institutional identity card. Borrowing privileges differ by category as defined in the Library Policy.

Counselling is confidential and covers mentoring, mental-health awareness and referral support. Page: mrdu.edu.in/student-life/counselling.

**Q. Where can I get a bonafide certificate?**

Bonafide certificates are issued by the department/administration on request. Apply through your department office with your name, roll number, programme, semester and purpose. Typical turnaround is a few working days; for status contact the department or +91 93481 61303.

**Q. How do I report sexual harassment?**

Contact the Internal Complaints Committee — Presiding Officer Dr. J. Anitha (Professor, CSE): 92983 03067; Convener Dr. T. Naga Durga (Associate Professor, EEE): 99853 70993; email internalcomplaintcell@mrdu.edu.in. Complaints are handled with confidentiality and due process under the UGC Regulations 2015 and MRDU ICC Policy.

**Q. What is the ICC?**

The Internal Complaints Committee implements UGC and statutory guidelines for a safe campus, handling complaints with confidentiality and due process. It follows the UGC (Prevention, Prohibition and Redressal of Sexual Harassment) Regulations, 2015 and the MRDU ICC Policy.

**Q. Is there an Equal Opportunity Cell?**

Yes — the Equal Opportunity Cell ensures equitable access to academic resources, scholarships and facilities, and coordinates awareness on diversity and inclusion. Downloads include the EOC Committee and EOC Policy.

**Q. Is there an SC/ST Cell?**

Yes — the SC/ST Cell facilitates scholarships, grievance support and compliance with reservation roster requirements in admissions and appointments. Page: mrdu.edu.in/student-life/sc-st-cell.

**Q. How do I get a Transfer Certificate?**

Transfer Certificates are issued on completion/withdrawal, after clearance of dues, return of library books and hostel clearance where applicable. Apply through the administration/examination office.

**Q. How do I get a Migration Certificate?**

Migration certificates are issued to graduating students on application to the examination section, along with other certificates, for candidates moving between boards or universities.

**Q. Who handles student welfare?**

Student welfare is coordinated by the Dean, Students Affair and Progression — Dr. J. Selwyn Babu (9791248068 / 9443148068) — covering mentoring, counselling, scholarships, grievance handling, hostel liaison, anti-ragging and ICC support, NSS/NCC, clubs and cultural activity.

**Q. How do I get my student ID card?**

Identity cards are issued after document verification and fee payment. The ID card is required for campus access, library borrowing, examinations (with the hall ticket) and hostel identification. Report a lost card to the administration for reissue.

**Q. What is the student portal?**

The student portal provides examination registration, hall tickets, results, RC/RV status, fee payments and academic records. Credentials are issued by the examination section/department after admission. Portal support: +91 93481 61303.

**Q. Is there an ombudsperson?**

Yes — the university publishes an 'Ombudsperson for Redressal of Grievances' document and a 'Grievance Committee & Ombudsperson' document under the Grievance Cell page, in line with UGC (Redressal of Grievances of Students) Regulations, 2023.

### Contact

**Q. Give me the university contact number.**

General enquiry: +91 93481 61303. Admissions: +91 87120 20867. Tirupati campus: 9247962726 / 9247962728 / 9247962729. Email: info@mrdu.edu.in.

**Q. What is the university email address?**

The general email is info@mrdu.edu.in. Function-specific addresses include grievancecell@mrdu.edu.in (grievances) and internalcomplaintcell@mrdu.edu.in (ICC).

**Q. What is the university address?**

Maisammaguda (H), Gundlapochampally Village, Medchal Mandal, Medchal-Malkajgiri District, Telangana State — 500100.

**Q. How do I contact the admissions office?**

Phone +91 87120 20867 or email info@mrdu.edu.in. You can also use the contact form at mrdu.edu.in/contact or the enquiry form at mrdu.edu.in/admissions/apply.

**Q. How do I contact the placement cell?**

Through the Training & Placement Cell — general line +91 93481 61303, email info@mrdu.edu.in, page mrdu.edu.in/about/placements, or your department placement coordinator.

**Q. How do I contact the examination section?**

+91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in, or your department examination coordinator.

**Q. How do I contact IT support?**

Through the IT cell via +91 93481 61303 / info@mrdu.edu.in. IT services and acceptable use are governed by the IT Policy.

**Q. How do I file an RTI request?**

Submit RTI requests to the Public Information Officer at Maisammaguda(H), Gundlapochampally Village, Medchal Mandal, Medchal-Malkajgiri District, Telangana 500100; email info@mrdu.edu.in; phone +91 93481 61303. Page: mrdu.edu.in/about/rti.

**Q. What are the office working hours?**

Administrative offices operate during standard institutional working hours on working days (Monday to Saturday), with the office schedule notified in the academic calendar. The Library reports 24X7 access.

### Documents

**Q. Where can I find the university brochure?**

The official MRDU Single Flyer is at mrdu.edu.in/uploads/2026/02/MRDU_Single-Flyer.pdf, and a 'Download brochure' link appears on the admissions page.

**Q. Where is the academic calendar?**

At mrdu.edu.in/academics/calendar, with the master document 'University Academic Calendar 2026-27' plus year-wise calendars for B.Tech, M.Tech, MBA, BBA and B.Com & B.Sc for 2026-2027 and 2025-2026.

**Q. Where are the academic regulations?**

Academic regulations are issued per regulation code (MR15 through MR25), approved by the Academic Council and published through the Academic Council page (mrdu.edu.in/about/academic-council) and department Syllabus tabs.

**Q. Where is the syllabus?**

Programme-wise syllabus is published on each department's 'Syllabus' tab. Programme detail pages note that the detailed syllabus is published by the department and updated each academic year.

**Q. Where is the fee structure document?**

At mrdu.edu.in/admissions/fees, with examination fees at mrdu.edu.in/examinations/fees.

**Q. Where is the mandatory disclosure?**

At mrdu.edu.in/about/mandatory-disclosure. A 'Mandatory Disclosure — Updated' notification is also published on the notifications feed under the Compliance category.

**Q. Where are the NAAC documents?**

At mrdu.edu.in/about/naac — SSR Cycle III (137 pages), IIQA Cycle III, IIQA Undertaking, IIQA Self Declaration, and AQAR reports for 2020-21, 2021-22, 2022-23 and 2023-24.

**Q. Where are the NIRF documents?**

At mrdu.edu.in/about/nirf — NIRF 2026 submissions for Overall, Engineering, Management, Innovation and SDG categories.

**Q. Where are the AQAR reports?**

The four AQAR reports (2020-21 to 2023-24) are published on mrdu.edu.in/about/naac.

**Q. Where are the policies?**

Policies are published across the section pages and the institutional document library — including Admission Policy (21 p), Student Code of Conduct (17 p), Faculty Code of Conduct (12 p), Anti-Ragging Policy, Grievance Policies, ICC Policy, Anti-Discrimination Policy, Gender Equality Policy, Library Policy (22 p), IT Policy (47 p), Green Campus Policy, Internship Policy (19 p), IPR Policy, NEP 2020 Policy, Alumni Policy and the SWAYAM-NPTEL Policy.

**Q. Where are the Ph.D. regulations?**

MRDU Ph.D Regulations 2025-26 is published under 'Latest Regulations' on mrdu.edu.in/research/phd.

**Q. Where is the placement report?**

Placement information is at mrdu.edu.in/about/placements; verified branch-wise and year-wise placement data is available from the Training & Placement Cell.

**Q. Where can I download previous question papers?**

At mrdu.edu.in/examinations/question-papers. Downloads require login credentials issued by the examination section; for papers not listed, contact your department examination coordinator.

### Events

**Q. What events are happening at MRDU?**

Published upcoming events include SPORTS ARENA 2026 (08-10 Oct 2026), AI HACK X MRDU 2K26 24-hour Hackathon (03-04 Oct 2026), the Women's Safety Awareness Session on POSH/POCSO (03 Oct 2026), VISHESH 2K26 National Inter-University Project Expo (18-19 Sep 2026), the Agri-Social and Women Entrepreneurship Cell inauguration (12 Sep 2026), SMART INDIA HACKATHON 2026 (02 Sep 2026) and the Careers in USA Fair (24 Aug 2026). See mrdu.edu.in/events.

**Q. What is SPORTS ARENA 2026?**

SPORTS ARENA 2026 is the university's sports event held from 08 to 10 October 2026 at the MRDU Sports Ground, organised at university level.

**Q. What is VISHESH?**

VISHESH is the university's National Inter-University Project Expo. VISHESH 2K26 was held on 18-19 September 2026; Vishesh 2K25 (the annual cultural fest) was held 24-25 September 2025.

**Q. What is AI HACK X MRDU?**

AI HACK X MRDU 2K26 is a 24-hour hackathon organised by the Department of CSE-AIML on 03-04 October 2026. Details and registration are on mrdu.edu.in/events/ai-hack.

**Q. What is KALOTSAVAM?**

KALOTSAVAM is MRDU's biggest cultural celebration, combining cultural programming with inter-department sports and technical quiz events. KALOTSAVAM 2K26 was held on 09 January 2026.

**Q. What is IGNITE?**

IGNITE is the university's Innovation & Entrepreneurship Summit where students and faculty showcase startups, prototypes and industry collaborations. IGNITE 2026 registration opened on 22 May 2026.

**Q. How do I register for an event?**

Each event card on mrdu.edu.in/events has a 'View event' action leading to the event detail with the registration link, eligibility, registration fee and organiser contact. Events can be filtered by status (upcoming/past) and department.

**Q. What is the ICSIC conference?**

ICSIC-2026 is the 1st International Conference on Sustainable Innovative Construction, held 24-25 April 2026, organised by the Civil Engineering department.

**Q. Are there entrepreneurship events?**

Yes — the EDC runs entrepreneurship programming including IGNITE 2026, the CYBERPRENEURS talk (21 Aug 2026), MSME Idea Hackathon 6.0, and the inauguration of the Agri-Social and Women Entrepreneurship Cells (12 Sep 2026).

**Q. Are there FDPs and workshops?**

Yes — published examples include the ATAL FDP on Next Generation EV's and AI-Battery Intelligence, the hands-on workshop 'IEEE Formatting Masterclass using Overleaf and Microsoft Word' (18 Jul 2026, R&D), and the workshop 'Git Smarter with Agentic AI' (18 Jul 2026, CSE-AIML).

**Q. What is the Smart India Hackathon participation?**

SMART INDIA HACKATHON 2026 was hosted on 02 September 2026 at MRDU, listed as a university-level event. A SIH 2026 banner is also published on the university homepage.

**Q. What is the graduation and alumni day?**

The 21st Graduation, Placement and Alumni Day was held on 25 July 2026 at the Major Auditorium. The 20th Graduation, Placement & Alumni Day was held on 12 July 2025 at MREC, before the deemed university transition.

## Structured Data Schema

Every live record carries: **Title · Category · Date · Programme · Department · Semester · Regulation · Deadline · Description · Document URL · Official URL · Status · Last Updated · Source**. Machine-readable files: `structured/live_records.json/.csv` (44 records), `structured/faculty.json/.csv` (449 records), `structured/contacts.json` (14 offices), `structured/hods.json`, `structured/documents_index.json` (162 documents).

## Answering Rules

1. **Official sources only** — cite the MRDU page or document for every answer.
2. **Time-qualify everything** — academic year, regulation code (MR15–MR25), semester, circular date, batch.
3. **Never invent** — if a value is not published, say so and give the office contact.
4. **Warn on payments** — fees only through official channels; never quote third-party bank details.
5. **Present conflicts openly** — give both figures with their sources.
6. **Route to humans** — every refusal includes the responsible office's phone and email.

## Answer Templates

**Static fact** — [Fact], as published by MRDU on [page]. Source: [URL].

**Indicative fee** — Published indicative tuition for [level] is ₹[amount] per year (source: Fees page, AY [year]). Programme- and category-specific amounts are confirmed by the admissions office — +91 87120 20867.

**Time-bound record** — [Title], published [date], for [programme] [semester] under regulation [MR-code]. Status: [active/closed/declared]. Details: [URL].

**Person lookup** — [Name] is [designation] in the [department] department. Profile: [URL]. Department contact: +91 93481 61303.

**Not published** — MRDU does not publish [value] on its official website. The verified figure is available from [office] — [phone], [email].

## Curriculum framework (applies to every programme)

Outcome-Based Education aligned to NBA and AICTE norms and to NEP 2020. Programme Outcomes (POs), Programme Specific Outcomes (PSOs) and Course Outcomes (COs) are defined for every programme. Syllabus is designed using concept maps, Bloom's Taxonomy and course-level learning objectives, delivered under the Choice Based Credit System (CBCS) with electives, and approved through the Board of Studies. Structured stakeholder feedback (students, alumni, employers, industry) drives syllabus revision. Regulations in use: **MR15, MR17, MR18, MR20, MR21, MR22, MR24, MR25**.

## Provenance and limits

- **Sourcing rule (checklist #39):** every fact above is taken from an official MRDU source — the website `mrdu.edu.in`, an official published document, or an official social account. Third-party sites, coaching blogs and aggregators were not used.
- **Conflicts are shown, not hidden** — for example CSE intake 960 (institutional table) against 720 (programme page).
- **124 of the 162 documents are scanned image PDFs** and are indexed by title and source page only; the remaining 38 are machine-readable.
- **Not published by MRDU** and therefore absent here: highest/average/median placement packages, hostel warden names, ward-wise intake splits, and dates that the university has not yet announced.
- Machine-readable companions: `structured/faculty.json` (449) · `structured/live_records.json` (44) · `structured/documents_index.json` (162) · `structured/contacts.json` (14) · `structured/hods.json` (10).

*End of the 100-page edition — 24 September 2026.*
